the restriction also touches compass targeting, spawn-field mutation and time/
weather behavior; ordinary startSleeping alone is not the completed route.
These are static primary facts, not observed gameplay results. On 2026-10-09
Root asks the maintainer through the asynchronous task UI: whether off-world
breathable rooms should support actual sleeping or retain the existing
restriction with only atmosphere refusal feedback. No response/authorization
is recorded at preparation. This unanswered state is superseded by
[the 2026-10-09 reply](v1.8.0-c18a-sleep/OWNER-DECISION-01.md). Sleep source and dimension/respawn policies remain unassigned.
The approved contract is not silently expanded by that question or inspection.

loaded heightmap and bounded boundary scan instead of SKY brightness. No original
airlock test, deadline, budget, registry, schema, network, save policy or asset
changes. Root clean build/2,179 JUnit, two clean DataGen runs and all 546 required

and each repaired native cohort retains 62 unwaived ERROR headers.

## Goal and scope

Deliver [Classic Content Completion](../versions/V1.8.0-CLASSIC-CONTENT-COMPLETION.md):
restore the classic machines, materials, items, armor, components, recipes,
models, sounds and progression in source batches, with provenance, hashes,
licenses and conversion records for every imported file, until the porting
matrix has no `UNKNOWN` and no in-scope `MISSING`. Development evidence does not
approve a release or remove inherited Required Gates.

```yaml
version: v1.8.0
status: IN_PROGRESS
phase: IMPLEMENTING
execution_state: ACTIVE
branch: codex/v1.8.0-classic-content
accepted_development_baseline: 55da6a58842762c382edce0a5a842d06bb76ff6e
runtime_implementation_allowed: true     # ADR-060..063 ACCEPTED on 2026-10-03; each later batch ADR first
runtime_build: 1.20.1-1.8.0-dev          # changed in C15a
last_updated: 2026-10-05
```

The v1.7 handoff is a development baseline, not a passed prerequisite. ADR-060
must separately authorize this version before any runtime change. Root is the
sole integration-checkout and central-file writer; the isolated same-version
worktree scopes below may have delegated writers. Independent review uses
read-only author/root access and fresh Temp outputs. The untracked user documentation bundle stays excluded from
reads and writes. No upstream code or asset is imported in C14.

  the [asset plan](v1.8.0-asset-plan.csv) (one handling per legacy asset), both
  checked by `scripts/validate_v180_content_ledger.py` and its unit tests (46
  after review round 2, plus 4 calibration tests of the derivation check).

  with player impact, earlier deferrals resolved, batch plan).
- The [coverage table](v1.8.0-contract-coverage.md) and porting-matrix rows per
  system.
- Independent contract review rounds until acceptance, then the evidence packet.

Explicit C14 non-goals: any change to production Java, resources, build
scripts or generated data; the runtime version; world migration; new items or
blocks; asset imports; candidate, tag or Gate changes; remote, long-load or
real-client tests.

Progress:

## C15a — materials, ores and the small plate press

Planned outputs (ADR-063 §1–§4 and §8):

- The material table of §1 (twelve materials, 86 registered entries), its tags,
  client tints and a creative tab; new art drawn as palette grids.
- Smelting, crafting, rolling and press recipes (§2); the small plate press with
  the piston exception (§3); the Overworld ores behind a server switch and a
  Forge biome modifier (§4); the v1.8 DataGen root and the superseded tool tags
  (§8); runtime `1.20.1-1.8.0-dev`.
- A0/A1 tests of §9 for C15a, the evidence packet, an independent
  implementation review.

Explicit C15a non-goals: the Moon, Mars and Venus surfaces and their ores
(C15b); exoplanet worlds (C15c); machines that make alloys, boules and crystals
(C16); world migration; imported assets; candidate, tag or Gate changes.

Progress:

- Implementation (`cb52c0b`, with `baa20f9` for two tests a mutation review
  found missing). Four points the accepted text did not settle became ADR-063
  revision 4, **proposed** (`f2006e8`): the v1.2 recipe `rolling_iron_bars`
  already takes iron ingots and the rolling machine refuses ambiguous inputs, so
  iron plates come from the press; rolling recipes name items because the
  kernel resolves ingredients before tags are bound; press tags resolve when it
  acts and an ambiguous match does nothing; the switch names.
- The thirteen `config:WORLDGEN.*` rows and `worldgen:CustomizableOreGen` moved
  to C15b (`d666aff`), which closes their Moon and Mars half.
- First GameTest run: two failures, both fixed before commit. Its log was
  overwritten by the rerun; the failure lines were
  `typedcapabilitiesrequireformationandretainresourcesacrossrebuild failed!
  Energy was lost on disassembly` (the v1.2 rolling capability fixture used gold
  ingots, which C15a now rolls; `920308e` stores diamonds, assertions unchanged)
  and `onlyarisingedgepresses failed! Expected 8 steel_plate, found 4` (the
  test placed a block on the first plates and pushed them away).
- Two art screens before commit changed the palette and the press top and
  bottom; the final screen is 19 `CLEAR` against both clients.
- Evidence run on `baa20f9`: 351 GameTests, 1,393 JUnit tests, clean DataGen
  diff ([C15a evidence](v1.8.0-c15a-materials/VERIFICATION.md)). After the run,
  four ledger-validator unit tests failed because their fixture tree lacked the
  batch evidence folders; `d8f80ed` fixed the fixture and the rerun passes.
- 17 ledger rows delivered: 15 IMPLEMENTED, 2 REDESIGNED.
- `V180MaterialArt` has 510 lines: 19 texture grids and the PNG encoder, one
  responsibility (the drawn art); it stays one class.
- Implementation review round 1 (`f95e851`): 0 Critical, 1 High, 3 Medium,
  3 Low, 7 Info; C15a accept with required changes, ADR-063 revision 4 accept
  with changes. Every finding is answered in its own commit
  ([dispositions](v1.8.0-c15a-materials/review-01-dispositions.md)): rutile no
  longer presses (H1), dilithium ore gives no mining XP (M1), kernel machine
  recipes refuse tag ingredients until C16a and ADR-061 revision 7 states it
  (M2), the press serializer, the ambiguity refusal, the ore tags and the
  Overworld numbers gained tests (M3, L1, L2), and the iron-plate paragraph
  states its condition, cost and the alternative. The sources JAR's
  difference was an empty untracked MDK directory in the working copy (I3).
  The packet was re-run on the fixed tree.
- Implementation review round 2 (`1a9f956`): 0 Critical, 0 High, 0 Medium,
  1 Low, 4 Info, no new defect; C15a accept, ADR-063 revision 4 and ADR-061
  revision 7 accept as written. The Low (network tag refusal tested only for
  rolling) and two Info notes are answered in their own commits
  ([dispositions](v1.8.0-c15a-materials/review-02-dispositions.md)); titanium,
  steel, silicon and alloy dusts need a source or an exemption in the C16d
  recipe graph (C15aR2-I2).
- Open C15 items: a client start with JEI installed and the JEI category on a
  real client (L3); the ADR-061 §4.5 human visual review of `coil_side`,
  `coil_top`, `storage` and the three press faces (I4).
- Known flakes outside C15a (I2): `debtandcreditsettleontheengine`
  (`LaserPhysicalGameTests`, v1.7) and
  `theskycontextisserverderivedsentonchangeandfollowseverychange`
  (`StationSkyContextGameTests`, v1.5) failed once in a review mutation run and
  passed in every clean run.
- Owner decisions open: ADR-063 revision 4 and ADR-061 revision 7, including the
  iron-plate route (the press, or re-keying `rolling_iron_bars`).

## C15b — the Moon, Mars and Venus surfaces

Planned outputs (ADR-063 §4 for the Moon and Mars ores, §5, §8, §9):

- Five surface blocks with drawn art; five biomes; the Moon as a noise Level of
  low relief (surface y 12–36) and Mars and Venus with their v1.4 routers and new
  surfaces; the crater, volcano and geode structures and the charred-tree
  feature; the Moon and Mars ore sets; five server switches; the 50 ledger rows
  moved to C15b, including the 13 `config:WORLDGEN.*` rows from C15a.
- A0/A1 tests of §9 for C15b, the S1 packaged-server check on an upgraded world,
  the evidence packet, an independent implementation review.

Explicit C15b non-goals: the Tau Ceti worlds and the crystal blocks (C15c); a
landing rule that puts Moon rockets on the ground (a later ADR); carvers on the
three bodies; restoring the legacy volcanic biome's creepers; world migration of
explored chunks; imported assets; candidate, tag or Gate changes.

Progress:

- Settled while implementing: ADR-063 revision 5, **proposed** (`9a8b0e0`,
  `7d5d60c`): two crater structures of one type, the Venus `patches` biome
  source (the copied v1.4 router has no climate to split the Venus biomes), no
  carvers on the three bodies (the legacy defaults had none), no spawns, the
  geode and charred-tree numbers, the five switches, the surfaces, the drops and
  tools (the charcoal log drops charcoal as the legacy log did; the geode shell
  needs an iron pickaxe until the C18b jackhammer) and a schema version on saved
  pieces. The geode shell texture is drawn new instead of imported
  (`495d758`, ADR-061 §4.2).
- A v1.7 GameTest timed the black-hole generator's idle re-check from a status
  that a new generator reads before it ever ticks; once its chunk started ticking
  late, the test measured that wait (31 ticks). `361c02b` waits for the first
  real evaluation; the 21-tick bound is unchanged.
- Fixed before the implementation commit, from GameTest runs: the Moon surface
  was one block low (the density's zero lies at S, the top block at S − 1); the
  volcano's skirt wrote below its box (the box now reaches the skirt and every
  write is clamped to it); the geode's half height truncated towards zero and
  reached past the radius (floor division); stale worldgen heightmaps on loaded
  chunks (tests read `WORLD_SURFACE`); Moon tests shared the fixed pad area
  (`MoonPadArea` gives it a known shape); the v1.3 atmosphere rooms on the Moon
  were buried by the new terrain (moved to y 64); heavy structure tests stalled
  the tests running beside them with synchronous chunk loads (they now load
  through tickets); and the surface test's rock check assumed a fixed filler
  depth (it now walks down the filler, which the vanilla surface depth and a
  crater rim can make up to about 18 blocks deep).
- GameTests stand in natural rock below the surface (the GameTest Overworld is a
  normal world, seed 0), and the new batches shift which test stands where. Two
  v1.7 GameTests failed once each before the implementation commit (an elevator
  ride cancelled as `ARRIVAL_OBSTRUCTED`, two items near a laser marker);
  `3d4cae8` made their messages name what was there. The evidence run on
  `3e24ddf` then failed three tests: the elevator zone test (gravel from the rock
  above filled the arrival platform; `2a74f90` roofs the anchor fixture), the C15a
  press switch test (its block was gone five ticks after a disabled press's rising
  edge, with no plates near it; cause unknown, `ef4bb85` makes it name what it
  saw), and the v1.5 sky-context test (the failed zone test's rider was still
  online). Logs: `failed-run-3e24ddf/` and `prechecks/` in the C15b packet.
- Implementation `d682e72`. S1 passed on its seventh attempt (the six before are
  kept): the third found that the release-test hook `sample` read unloaded
  columns as empty (`0744fba` loads them); the sixth passed but showed no
  structure start, because the retained v1.6 world has structures off. The
  harness now turns them on in its copy and requires starts, and `3e24ddf` adds
  a GameTest that each body's generator tries its own structure sets. Evidence
  run on `ef4bb85`: 370 GameTests, 1,419 JUnit tests, clean DataGen diff; S1 on
  a copy of the retained v1.6 world upgraded by the v1.7 handoff build ([C15b
  evidence](v1.8.0-c15b-surfaces/VERIFICATION.md)).
- 50 ledger rows delivered: 11 IMPLEMENTED, 39 REDESIGNED.

Planned outputs (ADR-063 §6, §8, §9 with revision 6):

- Two bodies in the Tau Ceti system with Levels, skies, routes and data
  satellite targets; six biomes; the lightwood tree, giant swamp tree, inverted

  independent implementation review.

Explicit C15c non-goals: mob spawns on either world (the legacy slimes and
creepers); lightning that strikes, burns or travels over the network; a
landing rule that clears plants (ADR-063 alternative D, left to a rocket-landing
ADR); the recipe graph check (C16d); imported assets; candidate, tag or Gate
changes.

Progress:

- Settled while implementing: ADR-063 revision 6, **proposed** (`f32ec0c`),
  with all seven textures drawn new (`315ccd8`).
- A GameTest of the C15c run found a C15b defect: a geode under low ground
  opened to the surface, since only its centre column kept four blocks of cover.
  `d0f9545` lowers each column's roof under its own ground, with a GameTest that
  digs a pit over a geode; ADR-063 revision 5 records the rule.
- C15c changes two v1.5/v1.7 GameTest expectations exactly: the celestial list
  now reports eleven bodies, and a warp quote to Tau Ceti no longer warns about
  missing rocket routes (the routeless warning is now checked on Cygnus X-1).
- The Tau Ceti path GameTest landed on the fifth fixed pad of Tau Ceti f; the
  first four were refused (the GameTest seed is random, C15bR1-M3, so whether
  for sea or plants is not known). A probe of the terrain noise over 1,000
  seeds put all eight pads under the sea in 89, and the jungle grass covers
  most forest columns. Revision 6 adds
  a landing ground of radius 160 around each world's origin: no Tau Ceti
  feature or alien forest grass starts inside it, and Tau Ceti f's ground there
  is raised above the sea (a `landing_ground_floor` density function and a
  `landing_ground` placement filter). The path test now requires the first
  pad, and a GameTest per world checks dry, clear ground within 32 blocks of
  each pad.
- The body names `body.advancedrocketrycommunity.tau_ceti_f` and `_g`, which
  the satellite terminal and star map read, were missing from the first
  language output; the resources test now requires them.
- C15b review round 1 (0 Critical, 1 High, 5 Medium, 6 Low, 6 Info) is answered
  commit by commit ([dispositions](v1.8.0-c15b-surfaces/review-01-dispositions.md)):
  geodes centred under the lowest ground over their lens, volcanoes refused over
  cliffs, the Moon's highland turf for foreign biomes and the upgrade band
  disclosed, structure tests that compare blocks before and after, the GameTest
  seed pinned to 0, server switches held in memory by tests, the Venus layout
  reason corrected, and the ledger, guide and ADR notes. Their re-run evidence is
  this slice's run.
- Implementation `28d24b8` (with `fae751b`, the landing ground in revision 6).
  Evidence run on `e39fd9d`: 384 GameTests, 1,441 JUnit tests, clean DataGen diff;
  S1 on a copy of the retained v1.6 world upgraded by the v1.7 handoff build, with
  the C15bR1 seam step ([C15c evidence](v1.8.0-c15c-worlds/VERIFICATION.md)).
- 25 ledger rows delivered: 18 IMPLEMENTED, 7 REDESIGNED.
- Open: one of six GameTest runs (before the seed was pinned) stalled the Tau
  Ceti path's transfer after the destination spawned, with no log line; five
  runs passed. The test now reports the journal phase, the journal state and
  whether the rocket's chunk ticks if it recurs.

(diagnostics and native warp restarts;
[verification](../work/v1.5.0-warp-native/VERIFICATION.md)). An independent review

### Former COMPLETION-PLAN checkpoint history

```text
# 至完成的分会话计划

更新：2026-09-30。v1.5 开发收尾（C1–C5）已在分支 `codex/v1.5.0-orbital-station-warp` 完成并推送；
v1.6 从 C6 开始，另开会话与分支。

本文件把从现在到 `v2.0.0` 的剩余工作拆成可以**一次一个会话**顺序完成的块，
避免单个会话上下文过大。块大小不求一致；每块结束时都有提交与推送，下一块
只需读本文件、`AGENTS.md` 要求的文件和对应版本文档即可开始。

本文件不是 Gate 批准，也不改变 `docs/04`、`docs/16` 的版本顺序与范围；
Codex/Claude 只能推进到 `READY_FOR_AUDIT`，`PASSED`/发布由维护者决定。

## 每个会话的固定做法

1. 读取 `AGENTS.md` 第 2 节要求的文件、本文件、当前版本文档、当前实施日志
   （`docs/work/<版本>-implementation-log.md`）顶部的“Segmented TODO”。
2. 只做本块范围；契约先行（ADR → 独立审核 → 接受 → 实现）。
3. 每个切片：Temp 证据目录 → 完整
   `gradlew clean build test runData runGameTestServer`（JAVA_HOME 为
   `C:/Program Files/Java/jdk-17.0.7`）→ 需要时原生专服 harness →
   `docs/work/<版本>-<切片>/` 证据包 → 校验器 → 提交 → 非强制推送。
4. 失败运行保留为证据；不删除、不放宽测试；不打 tag，不标记 PASSED。
5. 结束时更新本文件对应块的状态和“下一块”指针。

## 状态图例

`[ ]` 未开始 · `[~]` 进行中 · `[x]` 开发完成（待人工/审核）· `[H]` 需要维护者或真实硬件

依据 `docs/versions/V1.7.0-ENDGAME-SYSTEMS.md`。

### C10. v1.7 契约 `[x]`
- 以下各项的 ADR 送审：
  - 轨道激光/采矿；
  - 轨道炮与目标系统（安全范围）；
  - 黑洞发电；
  - 重力控制器；
  - 空间电梯物流（以 ADR-045 为基础）；
  - 权限、保护区、速率、能耗与审计日志。
- 结果（2026-10-01）：分支 `codex/v1.7.0-endgame-systems`，基线 `ab10fb5`。旧版审计只读、
  未导入。ADR-053..059 经四轮独立审核与一轮确认后由维护者确认接受：第 1 轮 0 Critical /
  3 High，第 2 轮 2 High，第 3 轮 1 High，第 4 轮无 Critical/High/Medium，5 个 Low 与确认轮的
  1 个 Info 均单独提交并写入正文。参考模型 27 项测试、26 个变异全部捕获；运行时未改动，
  重建 JAR 与 v1.6 交付构建字节一致（`docs/work/v1.7.0-preparation/`）。

### C11. 采矿激光、重力控制器与权限审计框架 `[x]`
- 结果（2026-10-02）：C11a 框架、C11b/C11c 激光钻（逻辑与物理模式）、C11d 区域重力控制器
  各有证据包（`docs/work/v1.7.0-c11a-framework/` 等）。独立实现审核第 1 轮 1 Critical / 2 High /
  5 Medium，逐项单独提交修复；第 2、3 轮四个切片全部接受，第 2 轮新增 2 个 Low 亦已修复
  （`docs/work/v1.7.0-c11-review/`，`6ace111` 处 1,304 个 JUnit、321 个 GameTest 通过）。
  不声明任何 Gate。

### C12. 黑洞能源、电梯物流、目标系统 `[x]`
- 结果（2026-10-02）：C12a 黑洞发电机、C12b 转运账本、C12c 轨道炮、C12d 空间电梯各有证据包
  （`docs/work/v1.7.0-c12a-black-hole/` 等）。独立实现审核第 1 轮 1 High / 3 Medium / 7 Low /
  6 Info，逐项单独提交修复；第 2、3 轮四个切片全部接受，第 2 轮新增 1 Low、6 Info 与第 3 轮
  1 Info 均已修复或记录接受（`docs/work/v1.7.0-c12-review/`，`10d9b12` 处 1,364 个 JUnit、
  335 个 GameTest 通过）。不声明任何 Gate。

### C13. v1.7 破坏边界、性能、恢复、原生证据、审核与交付 `[x]`
- 结果（2026-10-02）：端点破坏边界 GameTest；空闲端点跳过、索引与缓存等性能修复（空闲
  0.44 ms → 约 0.05 ms）；运维指南。独立审核第 1 轮 1 High / 4 Medium / 4 Low / 4 Info，第 2 轮
  新增 2 Medium / 2 Low / 1 Info，均逐项单独提交修复；修订后的原生线束另发现 2 个账本缺陷
  （存根永不清理、FULL 以下区块端点）并已修复；第 3 轮接受，无未关闭代码问题
  （`docs/work/v1.7.0-c13-closure/`）。原生专服：v1.6 世界升级、12 个轨道炮账本断点与源卸载、
  电梯货运断点、三种骑乘强制停止、参考负载。根刷写在负载下超出 60 ms 预算，需维护者决定
  写线程 ADR；ADR-054 §7 的每秒 16 次发射受持久化门限制，记为偏差。开发交付
  `docs/releases/v1.7.0/RELEASE-EVIDENCE.md`。V1/V2、S2 真实玩家与参考硬件仍为 `[H]`。
  不声明任何 Gate。

  - C16：机器家族、流体、储罐与泵；电弧炉、车床、切割机；结晶器、化学反应器、激光蚀刻机、离心机；
    组件、配方图工具与进度平衡（ADR-064 第 2 版已接受；两轮独立审核后技术项无未解决
    Critical/High/Medium，维护者已确认 controller 统一保存资源、氮气加入既有气态巨行星采集表；
    C16a-02 燃烧发电机已实现并通过 1,470 个 JUnit、401 个 GameTest 与专服两次重启/超限保存拒绝验证，

    太阳能；
  - C18：生命支持；宇航服装备、工具与附魔；天文台、数据处理器、进度与科技树；声音、OBJ 模型、天空与
```
